## Added 2026-11-03 — Bjernedehuse matr. 5a deed (hans-pedersen-parcel.md)
- S50: Sorø By og Birk skøde- og panteprotokol V.V, fol ~328–331, recorded 13 Nov 1884: skøde — estate of afdøde Bolsmand Hans Rasmussen & earlier-deceased Hustru Ane Marie Rasmussen sells Matr. No 5 A af Bjernedehuse, Bjernede Sogn (property "hidtil har tilhørt" Hans Rasmussen) to møller J.P. Mathiesen of Gyrstinge for 13,000 Kr; lease to Indsidder Hans Andersen from 12 Dec 1867 to be honored. Mortgage bond (fol 329/331): 3,600 Kr owed to umyndige Rasmus Peter og Ane Marie Rasmussen, children of afd. Bolsmand Hans Rasmussen og Hustru i Bjernedehusene (= P21, P22). Wife "Ane Marie Rasmussen" = Ane Rasmusdatter (P19) by INFERENCE (as S45). 1855 buyer of 5a (Realregister) = strong candidate same man; vol. T fol 97 not digitized. Matr. 5a as Hans Pedersen (P18) property REJECTED: https://arkivalieronline.rigsarkivet.dk/da/billedviser?bsid=147378 (shots skoede-5a-2026-11-03)

## Added 2026-11-03 — Scout MN Pack #4: Iron Range Research Center naturalization index (INDEX ONLY)

Finding aid: https://libguides.mnhs.org/naturalization/s2 → http://www.ironrangeresearchcenter.org/search
Film guide: https://libguides.mnhs.org/naturalization/s3
Index fields only: last/first, county, reel, volume, page. No year / first-vs-final / cert #. First name shows as HANS only unless noted. Confidence low–medium until microfilm. Free, no login. Scout: Records Scout MN pack #4.

- S74 (CANDIDATE index, NOT confirmed as P6): Iron Range Research Center naturalization index — PETERSEN, HANS, Redwood Co, reel 2, vol B, p 90. Best spelling match to tree surname Petersen. Detail: https://www.ironrangeresearchcenter.org/details/7/469643

- S145 (APPROVED — newspaper text; HIGH for printed content): Redwood Gazette (Redwood Falls), 1922-09-13, p.4, reprint from Morgan Messenger. https://www.loc.gov/resource/sn85025570/1922-09-13/ed-1/?sp=4 — OCR: https://tile.loc.gov/text-services/word-coordinates-service?segment=/service/ndnp/mnhi/batch_mnhi_eisa_ver02/data/sn85025570/00383347142/1922091301/0303.xml&format=alto_xml&full_text=1 . Printed name "Hans P. Petersen", age 55, of Brookville township, ~2 miles east of Brookville church; died suddenly ~4:30 "yesterday" afternoon of heart trouble and complications while sitting in a chair; had been ailing some time; funeral arrangements not yet made; survived by wife and several children (unnamed). Linked to P6. Calendar death date NOT proven by this article ("yesterday" is relative to the unshown Morgan Messenger issue date); 1922-09-06 remains CANDIDATE from MNHS index only.
- S146 (LOW — NOT LINKED): Redwood Gazette, 1922-09-20, p.7 — Jensen families motored to Evan (Brown Co) Monday for funeral of "H. P. Federson". https://www.loc.gov/resource/sn85025570/1922-09-20/ed-1/?sp=7 . Possibly OCR for Pederson/Petersen but different place; do not attach to P6.
- S147 (NEGATIVE): Redwood Gazette issues 1922-09-06 (https://www.loc.gov/resource/sn85025570/1922-09-06/ed-1/) and 1922-09-27 (https://www.loc.gov/resource/sn85025570/1922-09-27/ed-1/) scanned, 8 pp each — no Hans P. / Brookville / Dannebod / Maren hits (unrelated Petersons only). Sep 13 = S145; Sep 20 = S146. Keyword scan only; OCR garbles possible.
- CONFLICT OPEN: newspaper S145 prints Hans **P.** Petersen; MNHS death index lists Hans **T.** Petersen d. 1922-09-06 Redwood, cert 1922-MN-011514. See conflicts.md. Not resolved.
- Cleared for Storyteller / Tree Site: S145 death-notice facts only, as newspaper-sourced, with middle-initial conflict noted. Denmark Trip: nothing new.
- Newspapers claim: recommend keep HELD with Scout MN for Maren ~1937 / Ethel ~1929 / Olga ~1935 obits (Genealogist to confirm).

## Added 2026-11-03 — Kin Dig F: Ethel Dohrmann maiden (Gent vs Neubauer)
Pack: /workspace/genealogy/scout-kin-browser-FGH-2026-11-03.md (Dig F + Dig F residual birth). Free sources only.
- S148 (APPROVED — index; HIGH): MNHS People Search, Birth records — Ethel Bertha Neubauer, b. 1911-11-27, Brown County; mother's maiden GENT; cert 1911-03110 (only row for given Ethel*, exact DOB). https://www.mnhs.org/search/people → P32.
- S149 (APPROVED — index; HIGH): MNHS People Search, Death records — Ethel B. Dohrmann, d. 1994-06-07, Brown County; b. 1911-11-27; mother's maiden GENT; cert 1994-MN-015182 (Dohrman* spelling returns same single row). https://www.mnhs.org/search/people → P32.
- S150 (SECONDARY — corroborating only): Find a Grave 228250641, title "Ethel B Neubauer Dohrmann"; b. 27 Nov 1911 Sleepy Eye; d. 7 Jun 1994; Home Cemetery, Sleepy Eye; spouse Wesley Edward Dohrmann (m. 1935). No parents, no bio; created by Gary L. Schroeder Sr 2021-06-07. https://www.findagrave.com/memorial/228250641/ethel-b-dohrmann . Same memorial as S90; S90's Neubauer now confirmed by S148.
- Negatives (pack): MNHS death 1994 Ethel*/Neubauer* = 0; Ethel*/Gent* = 0 (expected). No MNHS marriage index for Wesley × Ethel ~1935.
- RESOLVED: Ethel's maiden = Neubauer; Gent = her mother's maiden (S148, S149). S89 Wesley bio "married Ethel Gent" = misstated maiden.
- Cleared for Storyteller / Tree Site: Ethel maiden Neubauer; mother's maiden Gent; b. 1911-11-27 Brown Co; d. 1994-06-07 Brown Co; note S89 bio said Gent.
- S151 (CANDIDATE — medium; name/age/year match S59, same person NOT proven): Haraldsted Sogn, Kontraministerialbog 1814–1842, Afgangsliste (departure list) 1833, entry no. 334 or 335 (number alignment unclear): "Ane Marie Hansd.", 28, occupation ditto under "Tjenestepige" (servant girl); date ditto under "1ste Mai 1833" (c. 1 May 1833). Destination (bestemmelsessted): "Ringsted" (HIGH — Dig 6 crop gate 2026-11-03, crops shots/scout-dk-dig6-2026-11-03/crop-img475-*.png). The "Grøftevandershov(?)" word Dig 5 kept as alternate belongs to the NEXT row (Inger Hansdatter ~15), not Ane Marie; dual-destination keep removed. Dig 6 crops confirm Row 335 Ane Marie → Ringsted; Grøfte… on Inger’s next row (shots/scout-dk-dig6-2026-11-03/). Earlier Dig 5 dual-destination uncertainty withdrawn for her row. AO bsid=150801 img 475, image id 25012697: https://api.rigsarkivet.dk/ao/v1/images/25012697 ; viewer https://arkivalieronline.rigsarkivet.dk/da/billedviser?bsid=150801 . About 1 month after S59 tilgang (arrival) 30 Mar 1833. Gaps: img 476–480 NEGATIVE (S156), img 481–483 NEGATIVE (S159, Dig 9; Nov 1834); Ringsted tilgang 1833 covered by S108 NEGATIVE nos 1–81; S157 (Dig 7) confirms S108 includes May 1833+ and May–Oct re-read NEGATIVE — Ringsted arrival gap OPEN (conflicts.md); Clausdatter vs Hansdatter stays OPEN (SUPERSEDED 2026-11-03: patronymic LOCKED Hansdatter by Alex; Clausdatter closed — see conflicts.md CLOSED section / Dig lock note at end of file). Supersedes S135 INCOMPLETE (afgang volume now found).

## Added 2026-11-03 — Scout MN Pack #8: Ethel / Olga / Maren newspapers

- S152 (APPROVED — newspaper text, HIGH for printed content; gate re-fetch of LOC page timed out 2026-11-03, relies on Scout OCR excerpt in pack #8): Redwood Gazette, 6 Nov 1929, p.5 — “FUNERAL THURSDAY FOR MRS. WERNER JENSEN.” Ethel Mathilda Petersen, b. Brookville Twp 15 Jan 1902; m. Werner Jensen 3 Jan 1923; d. Redwood Falls hospital 20 Oct 1929 after operation; funeral Hans Jensen home Brookville; Brookville Lutheran; burial church cemetery; Rev. H. Madsen Sleepy Eye. Survived by mother Mrs. Hans Petersen of Brookville; brothers Nels (Cobden), Rudolph (Gilfillan), Louis (Golden Gate), Elmer (at home); sisters Mrs. Herman Hansen (Roberts WI), Mrs. Sam Strodimier (Circle MT), Olga (at home). https://www.loc.gov/resource/sn85025570/1929-11-06/ed-1/?sp=5 — DOB matches S92.
- S153 (NEGATIVE / INCOMPLETE — P10 Olga 1935; claim held): Chronicling America JSON 1935 timed out; Gazette 1935-01-02 404; no 1935 obit found this pass. Not a full-year scan.
- S154 (NEGATIVE / INCOMPLETE — P7 Maren 1937; claim held): JSON 1937 timed out; Gazette 1937-01-06 404; Hub unused this pass. No 1937 obit found.
- S155 (CANDIDATE — MED corroborating only; identity Mrs. Hans Petersen = P7 probable, not proven by text): Same S152 page — “Mrs. Hans Petersen of Brookville” surviving mother of Ethel, Nov 1929. Supports widow living after Hans 1922; does not prove Maren Sophia given names or 1937 death.

### Dig H (2026-11-03) — Hans Rasmussen (P20) birth year ~1801 vs ~1831, S170–S173
Pack: scout-kin-digH-2026-11-03.md; shots/scout-kin-digH-2026-11-03/ (S33/S38/S46 crops; DDD table + S38 year zoom taken off-box only, not on box). Free only. Both sides kept; no silent winner.

Digit shapes match 1808/1843 on same page; no "3" form seen. Gate spot-checked crop s38_groom_year_mid.png: reads "1801 · 19 April". ### S344 — HIT HIGH — Sleepy Eye Herald-Dispatch obituary 26 Dec 1946
Person: Henry F. Dohrmann (P44).
Claim (verbatim extracts):
- Died Sunday ~7 a.m. Municipal hospital after fall at home West Walnut; son Wesley found him.
- Funeral Tue home 1:30 / Methodist church 2; Rev. Hillis Slaymaker; Home cemetery.
- **“Henry F. Dohrmann was born in Hanover, Germany, October 15, 1864, and came to America with his parents in 1873. They lived near Chicago for two years, then came to Brown county… Leavenworth township…”**
- Confirmed Lutheran age 13; m. Ida Diederich 7 Nov 1889 German Methodist, Rev. Lehnert; golden wedding 1939.
- Ten children; Edwin d. infancy; living Arthur; Stella Bittner Wabasso; Elsie George Sleepy Eye; Lucy Lambrecht Eden; Wesley; Verna Ingraham; Henry Jr.; Elmer; Alice Murphy. Sister **Mrs. Albert Durbahn**.
- Organized first co-op shipping assoc. with Robert Frank; Farmers elevator director 28 yrs / president.
URL (viewer): https://newspapers.mnhs.org/jsp/PsImageViewer.jsp?doc_id=e30dccc6-90c4-4606-85bd-3940f40d10c3%2Fmnhi0031%2F1B19825E%2F46122601
